In-process test hooks are the `test_hooks` Cargo feature and the `S3A_KITE_TEST_HOOKS` environment-variable control channel, with a closed hook-point list; release builds reject the variables (F-01, DD-151, AT-222). Coverage and `break_index` apply only in an Edge file that contains type-70 records; a hyper-tile or Unsigned-Edge valid floor of a file that is not unauthenticated as a whole is trusted (F-02). Minors: chain-inactive label-table `unsealed` (F-03); well-typed unused fields ignored, malformed is `invalid_param` (F-04); unpinned embedded-key hash mismatch is `signer_not_pinned` (F-05); `chain_break` in DD-145's outcome list (F-06); DD-146 normalize-then-unique-root and AT-52 cross-root (F-07); DD-146 config/state list matches §5.2.2 (F-08); AT-201(8) lead-in removed (F-09); silent skip writes nothing to `--log-dir` (F-10); host crate at `crates/s3a-kite`, out of workspace (F-11); AT-169/AT-181 root-runner skip (F-12); oversize download is `download_conflict`, inclusion index not subject to `--max-open-bytes`, `--max-fetch-bytes` ≤ `--max-open-bytes` (F-13, AT-223, AT-224); startup canonicalize/lstat before lock creation (F-14); covering ATs for dedupe, LRS extensions, deny-list literals, and pre-create working-archive `404` (F-15, AT-225, AT-226). Acceptance tests: AT-01–AT-226. Draft decisions: DD-01–DD-151 (DD-63 retired; DD-126 unused). Open questions: Q-01–Q-18. v20 itself is not frozen (DD-149).
*   2026-11-16: v21. Addresses goldfish round 20 (F-01–F-18; 0 blockers, 3 majors, 15 minors). The host re-opens the working archive in every step-0/1 hold and applies a name-binding `fstatat(..., AT_SYMLINK_NOFOLLOW)` check immediately before each tile-10 write; hook `pause_before_name_binding_check` sits just before that check; no startup-lifetime fd (F-01, AT-219, AT-203). Newly-observed LRS IPs are a unit test of `lrs_deny_union`; an `https` LRS hostname that fails to resolve at start is a startup error (F-02, AT-226). The `window_issue_select` hook is self-contained: after `/health`, the window fetches candidates and selects the first, with the Bearer token (F-03, AT-125). Minors: `snapshot_too_large` at publish (F-04, AT-227); `open_verified` rejects contradictory reports (F-05); header reads use the hold's fd (F-06); parent-dir `fsync` and `lrs-retry/` mode `0700` (F-07); floor-transition withholding ATs (F-08); control-fixture pixmap oracle, never OCR (F-09); unknown-layout type-64 and BLOB floors paint metadata only (F-10); startup delete hooks (F-11); post-`/health` byte-identity baseline (F-12); AT-183 parse function and AT-168 first-view wording (F-13); name-based `metadata.google.internal` deny (F-14); header and LRS-body caps tied to existing flags (F-15); covering cases on AT-172 / AT-125 / AT-171, `render_failed` untestable on purpose (F-16); `S3A_KITE_BIN*` feature-build matrix (F-17); missing recorded snapshot is Q-19 (F-18). Acceptance tests: AT-01–AT-227. Draft decisions: DD-01–DD-151 (DD-63 retired; DD-126 unused). Open questions: Q-01–Q-19. v21 itself is not frozen (DD-149).
*   2026-11-16: v22. Addresses goldfish round 21 (F-01–F-19; 0 blockers, 3 majors, 16 minors). Working-archive create is startup-only: AT-79, AT-74, AT-53, the `write_failed` row, and §5.6 match that model; a failed startup create is a startup error naming `--working-archive` (F-01, AT-78). Fetch-plus-hook ATs and AT-227 run on `egress_test` builds; `S3A_KITE_BIN_EGRESS_TEST_HOOKS` is populated (F-02). Hook `pause_before_lrs_retry_write` makes AT-156 case 3 deterministic (F-03). Ashley answered Q-19 (2026-11-16, DD-152): a missing recorded snapshot is a re-fetch, not permanent `snapshot_invalid`; a hash mismatch publishes the new sha and appends a new index line (AT-228). Minors: `snapshot_too_large` download and waiter outcomes (F-04); 413 header-block before authentication (F-05); `invalid_view` precedes a missing `handle`, and `/v1/frame` `instant` is a query literal (F-06); `withheld_count` includes mark-only records (F-07); BLOB/PROVENANCE and undecodable hyper-tile floors are metadata-only in §5.3 (F-08); `open_verified` rejects `break_index: None` with `chain_break` / `signature_known_signer` / `crc`, and hyper-tile `verified` / `self_signed` (F-09); a failed runtime `revoke-pending` unlink is logged, returns 200, and is handled at the next start (F-10); `drive_path` is lexically normalized and walked from the unique allowed root (F-11); AT-183 unit vs host-spawn split (F-12); AT-226 observes DNS via a counting stub resolver in a network namespace (F-13); bytewise candidate-path sort and observable `page_path` (F-14); AT-66 covers both size flags (F-15); AT-187 plants stale vs locked temps in the host's own snapshot dir (F-16); AT-117 / AT-142 other-owner skip (F-17); AT-125's `0.0.0.0` without `--headless` runs on `S3A_KITE_BIN` (F-18); AT-46 in-place bit clear, step-3 flag check under the mutex, history reordered (F-19). Acceptance tests: AT-01–AT-228. Draft decisions: DD-01–DD-152 (DD-63 retired; DD-126 unused). Open questions: Q-01–Q-19 (Q-19 answered). v22 itself is not frozen (DD-149).
*   2026-11-16: v23. Addresses goldfish round 22 (F-01–F-13; 0 blockers, 2 majors, 11 minors). `AcademicPaperRecord` candidates keep `page_path` `null`; the duplicate-winner corpus file is exposed as `archive_path` (F-01, AT-225, AT-101). The §5.8 pixel oracle is a render-model control of the same archive bytes: withheld field values are replaced after decode, before paint; derived digests, CRCs, and seal/signature status stay equal (F-02). Stale "recorded snapshot, no fetch" restatements follow DD-152; usability and `snapshot_invalid` look only at the last recorded sha (F-03, F-04, AT-228). Step-0 2b runs when there are bytes to deliver, including after a re-fetch publish (F-05). A qualifying `on` unlinks leftover `revoke-pending` before persisting `on`; a failed that unlink is fail-closed (F-06, AT-229). Every `s3a-kite-tmp-*` temp is `LOCK_EX` on its own fd (F-07). Closed `fetch_error` vocabulary (F-08). Severity badges are `SemanticNode` labels with a CSS rect (F-09, AT-19). Unreadable working-archive headers are `write_failed`; `lrs-retry` must be a real directory (F-10). `--handle-idle-secs` bounds unauthenticated header/idle time; a connection cap is Q-20 (F-11, AT-230). Covering cases on AT-77, AT-117, AT-224, AT-68, AT-103, and AT-222's feature-build half; DD-121 and `download_conflict` wording (F-12). Leftover snapshot-size wording cites AT-170 (F-13). Acceptance tests: AT-01–AT-230. Draft decisions: DD-01–DD-152 (DD-63 retired; DD-126 unused). Open questions: Q-01–Q-20 (Q-19 answered). v23 itself is not frozen (DD-149).
*   2026-11-16: v24. Addresses goldfish round 23 (F-01–F-18; 0 blockers, 2 majors, 16 minors). One-shot hook action `fail_once_with_errno=N`; AT-229 case 1, AT-212, and AT-213 use it so a later request in the same process can succeed (F-01). Hook `pause_before_consent_rename` makes AT-187's consent-temp case reachable (F-02). The §5.5 source-decision paragraph looks only at the last index line (F-03). DD-57, DD-148, and AT-35 use `break_index` = (highest preceding authentic `tile_index`) + 1, or 0 (F-04). `torn_tile_indices` contains only the torn predicate (F-05, AT-165). The host `fstat`s a request-path fd and the pack `pread`s from offset 0 (F-06). Hyper-tile reports may not use `signer_not_pinned` (F-07, AT-211). Oversized LRS responses follow the status class when a status line is known, else permanent; a retry-file write failure is `failed` and is not fail-closed (F-08, AT-191). `--handle-idle-secs` runs from TCP accept and includes the TLS handshake (F-09, AT-230). Edge floor types other than 64/70/71 are metadata-only (F-10, AT-232). AT-172's `floor-detail` request supplies `floor` (F-11). Hook `pause_after_consent_receipt` makes AT-198's interleavings deterministic; AT-198 uses `S3A_KITE_BIN_TEST_HOOKS` (F-12). Snapshot temps are sealed on the same fd that holds `LOCK_EX` (F-13). The reader test crate links the pack's `s3a-engine` (F-14). Sidecar `.lock` opens use safe-open (F-15, AT-208). Startup temp cleanup never deletes a configured working-archive or consent path; those basenames must not match `s3a-kite-tmp-*` (F-16, AT-208). Covering cases on AT-171, AT-88, AT-183, AT-191, and AT-151 (F-17). DD-93 names token and TLS files; DD-130 includes the unsigned tail (F-18). Q-20 is answered as DD-153: a fixed cap of 8 concurrent connections on a non-loopback bind; connection 9 is accept-then-close (AT-231). Acceptance tests: AT-01–AT-232. Draft decisions: DD-01–DD-153 (DD-63 retired; DD-126 unused). Open questions: Q-01–Q-20 (Q-19 and Q-20 answered). v24 itself is not frozen (DD-149).

*   2026-11-16: v24.1. Records Ashley's 2026-11-16 build answers (Q-01–Q-18; Q-19 and Q-20 were already in v24). Token file kept (Q-01). Lab LRS endpoint `https://34.74.163.88.sslip.io/xapi/` (xAPI 1.0.3); consent copy drafted in §5.2.3, pending Ashley and IRB (Q-02, DD-158). `pulldown-cmark` with tables, and a narrow Markdown-image exception for academic papers (Q-04, DD-13, DD-118, AT-233). No CJK/emoji packs in the first build (Q-05, DD-35). Phone budgets and `max_frame_pixels` / `max_open_bytes` will be set from Pixel 9 measurements; no invented numbers (Q-06, Q-17). Select and download are separate existing verbs; select is no longer the inclusion decision; deselect is logged without a reason, snapshot, fingerprint, or corpus entry; Pass 1 skips stay unrecorded (Q-07, DD-154, AT-83, AT-234, AT-235). Lab researcher IDs are the user's username or email; a study has a study ID and participant codes such as `P-017` (Q-08, DD-155). LRS retries are 3 tries, 10 seconds apart, in the background; only error code and message may be sent off for diagnosis (Q-09, DD-51, DD-156, AT-236). Severity colours are Okabe-Ito `#0072B2` / `#E69F00` / `#D55E00` with labels always (Q-10, DD-07, AT-19). Product rename to S3A Navigator (`s3a-navigator`) is recorded; the in-tree rename is a later build step. The GitHub fork is now [ashleycribb/s3a-Navigator](https://github.com/ashleycribb/s3a-Navigator) (Q-11, DD-54). Field name `source_url` (Q-12). Actor homepage is built by the host, not a flag (Q-13, DD-157, AT-237). Timed cargo-fuzz is 300 seconds per push plus seed replay (Q-14, AT-71). Seal-stripped remnants are tagged; repair is restore-from-verified-copy only, never a re-seal; otherwise unrepairable; the header flag stays deferred (Q-18, DD-159, AT-238). Q-15 and Q-16 stay future. Round-24 minors F-01–F-14 are scheduled in `BUILD-PLAN.md`; F-13 (fd-based snapshot API in s3a-engine) is a write-path dependency (AT-239). Ashley authorized a local test-first build, and a push of a green step to a build branch on the Navigator fork (never `main`). Formal confirmation of DD-09 and DD-105 remains outstanding; DD-09's select-equals-inclusion wording is corrected. Acceptance tests: AT-01–AT-239. Draft decisions: DD-01–DD-159 (DD-63 retired; DD-126 unused). Open questions: Q-01–Q-20 (all answered or future).
